                                                      Corporate Introduction
                                                            公司簡介

Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited (“Kingdee International” or “Kingdee”) was established in 1993. It is listed on the Main Board of the
Hong Kong Stock Exchange (stock code: 0268.HK) and headquartered in Shenzhen, China. Adhering to the core values of “Acting in all Conscience, with
Integrity and Righteousness”, the Company is committed to helping businesses achieve their growth targets through dedicated services. It strives to provide
clients with the most trusted enterprise service platform.

According to IDC, Kingdee ranked as No.1 in the SaaS ERM (Enterprise Resource Management Cloud Service) for Large, Medium and SME enterprises
in China, maintaining the No.1 in the market share in terms of SaaS EA (Enterprise Application Software Cloud Services) and Finance Cloud. Meanwhile,
Kingdee has become the only Enterprise SaaS vendor in China that has entered into the top five of Gartner’s application platform software in 2020, as well as
the only Chinese vendor to be selected in IDC’s Asia-pacific manufacturing ERP SaaS MarketScape, as a Contender. In addition, Kingdee Cloud was selected
in “The State of Low-Code Platforms of Forrester”, having the most case studies among all enterprise PaaS+SaaS platforms. Kingdee has attached great
importance to user privacy and data security, and obtained the “grand slam” of security and privacy certifications, including ISO27001, ISO27701, Grade III
Protection of Information Security, Cloud Security Alliance CSA-STAR.

Many cloud service products of Kingdee have won the favour of benchmark enterprises, including Kingdee Cloud Cosmic (new-generation PaaS Platform for
enterprises), Kingdee Cloud Constellation (SaaS solution for large enterprises), Kingdee Cloud Galaxy (SaaS solution for medium-sized enterprises), Kingdee
Cloud Stellar (SaaS solution for micro and small enterprises). Kingdee has provided software management and cloud services for more than 6.8 million
enterprises, governments and organizations across the world.

金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司（「金蝶國際」或「金蝶」
                       ）始創於1993年，是香港聯交所主板上市公司（股票代碼：0268.HK），總部位於
中國深圳。以「致良知、走正道、行王道」為核心價值觀，以「全心全意為企業服務，讓陽光照進每一個企業」為使命，致力成
為「最值得託付的企業服務平台」。

IDC數據顯示，金蝶國際在中國大、中、小型企業SaaS ERM（企業資源管理雲服務）市場均排名第一，以及在SaaS EA（企業級應
用軟件雲服務）、財務雲市場佔有率維持排名第一。同時，金蝶成為Gartner 2020年應用平台軟件前五的唯一中國企業級SaaS廠
商；入選IDC 2021亞太區製造ERP SaaS競爭力象限的中國廠商，位居挑戰者(Contenders)象限；入選「Forrester低代碼分析報告」並
成為報告內實踐案例最多的企業級PaaS+SaaS平台。金蝶高度重視使用者隱私和數據安全，已完成產品安全及隱私體系「大滿
貫」，包括取得了ISO27001、ISO27701、國家信息安全等級保護三級認證、國際雲安全聯盟CSA-STAR等多項安全認證。

金蝶旗下的多款雲服務產品獲得標杆企業的青睞，包括金蝶雲·蒼穹（新一代企業級PaaS平台）、金蝶雲·星瀚（大型企業SaaS管理
雲）、金蝶雲·星空（高成長型企業SaaS管理雲）、金蝶雲·星辰（小微企業SaaS管理雲）等，已為世界範圍內超過680萬家企業、政府
等組織提供數字化管理解決方案。

Outlook:                                                                                        展望：

In 2022, Kingdee will join hands with ecosystem partners to launch a full-                      2022，金蝶將攜手生態夥伴，發起全面進攻，重點突

     Credit risk                                                                                 信貸風險

     Credit risk arises from cash and cash equivalents, contractual cash flows of debt           信用風險源於現金及現金等價物、以攤餘成本
     instruments carried at amortised cost, contractual cash flows of debt instruments           計量的債務工具合同現金流量、以公允價值計
     carried at fair value through profit or loss (FVPL) and deposits with banks and             量且其變動計入損益的債務工具合同現金流
     other financial institutions, as well as credit exposures to customers, including           量、銀行和其他金融機構存款以及未收應收款
     outstanding receivables.                                                                    在內的客戶信用風險。

     The management manages the credit risk of cash and cash equivalents, pledged                為管理來自現金及現金等價物、抵押存款、短
     and short-term bank deposits, long-term bank deposits and asset management                  期銀行存款、長期銀行存款及資產管理產品(歸
     products (classified as financial assets at FVPL) by only transacting with state-           類為以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融
     owned financial institutions and reputable commercial banks which are all high-             資產)的信用風險，本集團管理層僅與中國國有
     credit-quality financial institutions in the PRC and Hong Kong.                             或信譽良好的中國大陸及香港的財務機構進行
                                                                                                 交易。

     In relation to trade receivables and contract assets, the Group has two kinds of            對於應收賬款及合同資產，本集團有兩種銷售

Share Award Scheme                                                                                  股份獎勵計劃

The share award scheme (the “Share Award Scheme”) was adopted by the Company on 4                    本公司二零一五年十二月四日採納之股份獎勵計劃
December 2015 to provide incentives and reward the contributions of certain employees               （
                                                                                                    「股份獎勵計劃」）以提供激勵以及獎勵本集團若干僱
and Directors of the Group. The Share Award Scheme shall be valid and effective for a                員及董事所作的貢獻。股份獎勵計劃自採納之日起有
period of 10 years commencing from the adoption date. The Board may, from time to                    效期為十年。董事會經考慮彼等認為合適的多項因素
time, in its absolute discretion select the participants after taking into various factors as        後可不時全權酌情挑選獲選參與者，並釐訂授予各獲
they deem appropriate and determine the number of award shares to be awarded to each                 選參與者的獎勵股份數目。獎勵股份將包括受託人使
of the selected participants. The awarded shares will comprise of Shares subscribed for              用以本公司自有資金為受託人準備的現金認購或購買
or purchased by the trustee out of cash arranged by the Company out of the Company’s                 的股份，受託人將以信託形式代獲選參與者持有獎勵
funds to the trustee and be held on trust for the selected participants until such Shares are        股份，直至該等股份根據股份獎勵計劃的條文歸屬予
vested with the selected participants in accordance with the provisions of the Share Award           獲選參與者為止。
Scheme.

Board Diversity Policy                                                                        董事會多元化政策

The Company has adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board Diversity Policy”) since          本公司已於二零一三年八月採納董事會成員多元化
August 2013. The policy sets out the approach to achieve diversity in the Board which         （
                                                                                              「董事會多元化政策」 ）政策。政策列載董事會為達致
will include and make good use of the differences in skills, backgrounds and industry          成員多元化而採取的方針，其中將涵蓋且善用董事會
experience, gender, knowledge and other qualities of the members of the Board. These           各成員所具備不同的技能、背景及行業經驗、性別、
differences will be considered in determining the optimum composition of the Board and         知識以及其他特質。在組成最理想的董事會時，將考
all Board appointments will be based on merit, having due regard to the overall effective      慮上述各方面的差異，而董事會所有委任均以用人唯
functioning of the Board as a whole. The Company believes that diversity can strengthen        才為原則，且顧及到董事會整體的職能可有效發揮。

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated financial     我們的目標，是對合併財務報表整體是否不存在由於
statements as a whole are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error,      欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保證，並
and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion solely       出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們僅向閣下（作
to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or        為整體）報告我們的意見，除此之外本報告別無其他
accept liability to any other person for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance     目的。我們不會就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上
is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance     或承擔任何責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能
with ISAs will always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can         保證按照《國際審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯
arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate,    誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤
they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on        引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響合
the basis of these consolidated financial statements.                                          併財務報表使用者依賴合併財務報表所作出的經濟決
                                                                                               定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。

As part of an audit in accordance with ISAs, we exercise professional judgment and             在根據《國際審計準則》進行審計的過程中，我們運用
maintain professional scepticism throughout the audit. We also:                                了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。我們亦：

•      Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the consolidated financial    •     識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致合併財務報
       statements, whether due to fraud or error, design and perform audit procedures                表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及執行審計
       responsive to those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and                   程序以應對這些風險，以及獲取充足和適當的
       appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material          審計憑證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可
       misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from error, as             能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或
       fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or           淩駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐而
       the override of internal control.                                                             導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯
                                                                                                     誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險。

•      Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit in order to design    •     了解與審計相關的內部控制，以設計適當的審

       of expressing an opinion on the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control.                性發表意見。

       of accounting estimates and related disclosures made by the directors.                        估計和相關披露的合理性。

       basis of accounting and, based on the audit evidence obtained, whether a material             論。根據所獲取的審計憑證，確定是否存在與

2.2   Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                          2.2   合併與權益會計原則

2.2.1 Subsidiaries                                                                               2.2.1 子公司

      Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which the Group has           子公司為本集團所控制的實體（包括結構化實
      control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights               體）。當本集團從或有權從與該實體的參與中獲
      to, variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect          得變動收益並有能力對這些收益產生影響時，
      those returns through its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are         則其控制該子公司。自控制權轉移至本集團之
      fully consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the Group. They              日起，子公司被合併。其自喪失實際控制權之
      are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                                            日起停止合併。

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                             本集團的業務合併採用會計購買法核算（見附註
      combinations by the Group (refer to Note 2.3).                                                   2.3）
                                                                                                          。

      Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on transactions                        集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及交易的未變現
      between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated                    利得予以對銷。未變現損失亦予以對銷，除非
      unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.              交易提供所轉撥資產的減值證據。子公司報告
      Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                  的數額已按需要作出改變，以確保與本集團採
      consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                                              用的政策符合一致。

      Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries are shown                    子公司業績和股權中的非控股權益分別列示於
      separately in the consolidated income statement, consolidated statement of                       合併利潤表、合併綜合收益表、合併股權變動
      comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and consolidated statement                  表和合併財務狀況表。
      of financial position respectively.

2.2.2 Associates                                                                                 2.2.2 聯營

      Associates are all entities over which the Group has significant influence but not               聯營公司是集團對其有重大影響但不控制或聯
      control or joint control. This is generally the case where the Group holds between               合控制的所有實體。通常情況下，該集團擁有
      20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are accounted for                    20%至50%的投票權。聯營公司的投資在最初
      using the equity method of accounting (see Note 2.2.3 below), after initially being              按成本確認後，以權益會計方法核算（見附註
      recognised at cost.                                                                              2.2.3）
                                                                                                            。

2.2.3 Equity method                                                                              2.2.3 權益法

      Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially recognised                  權益法下，投資賬面價值最初以成本確認，之
      at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the post-                      後卻按本集團持有投資對象得份額確認投資利
      acquisition profits or losses of the investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s                 潤或虧損以及其他綜合收益。從聯營公司收到
      share of movements in other comprehensive income of the investee in other                        或應收的股息確認為投資賬面價值的減少。
      comprehensive income. Dividends received or receivable from associates are
      recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.

      Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment equals                       若本集團在股本投資中所承擔的損失與本集團
      or exceeds its interest in the entity, including any other unsecured long-term                   在該實體（包括任何其他無擔保的長期應收賬
      receivables, the Group does not recognise further losses, unless it has incurred                 款）的權益相等或超過其權益，則本集團不承認
      obligations or made payments on behalf of the other entity.                                      進一步的損失，除非本集團已為該實體承擔了
                                                                                                       義務或代表該實體支付了款項。

2     Summary of significant accounting policies                                              2     重要會計政策摘要（續）
      (Continued)

2.2   Principles of consolidation and equity accounting (Continued)                           2.2   合併與權益會計原則（續）

2.2.3 Equity method (Continued)                                                               2.2.3 權益法（續）

      Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group and its associates are                     本集團與其聯營企業和合資企業之間的交易的
      eliminated to the extent of the Group’s interest in these entities. Unrealised losses         未變現收益，按本集團對這些實體的份額予以

2.3   Business combinations                                                                   2.3   企業合併

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                      不論權益工具或其他資產是否被收購，會計的
      combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are                    購買法都被用於所有的企業合併。為收購附屬
      acquired. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary                   公司而轉讓的代價包括：
      comprises the:

      •      fair values of the assets transferred                                                  •    轉讓資產的公允價值

      •      liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business                     •    對被收購企業的前所有者承擔的責任

      •      equity interests issued by the Group                                                   •    集團發行的權益

      •      fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent consideration         •    或有對價安排產生的任何資產或負債的
             arrangement, and                                                                            公允價值，以及

      •      fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                      •    子公司現有權益的公允價值

      Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed in a          企業合併中取得的可辨認資產、負債和或有負
      business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair           債除個別情況外，最初按取得日的公允價值計
      values at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-controlling interest             量。本集團按按公允價值或按非控股權益在被
      in the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either at fair value or         收購實體的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比例，按逐
      at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net            筆收購的方式確認其在被收購實體中的任何非
      identifiable assets.                                                                          控股權益。

      Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                           購買相關成本在產生時支銷。

      The excess of the:                                                                            過量的：

      •      consideration transferred                                                              •   支付的對價

      •      amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired entity, and                     •   收購實體中任何非控制權益的金額，以及

      •      acquisition-date fair value of any previous equity interest in the acquired            •   被收購實體之前任何股權的收購日公允價值
             entity

      over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is recorded as goodwill.          超過公允價值的淨可辨認資產收購記為商譽。
      If those amounts are less than the fair value of the net identifiable assets of the           如果該等金額低於被收購企業的可辨認資產淨
      business acquired, the difference is recognised directly in profit or loss as a               值的公允價值，則差額直接確認為廉價收購的
      bargain purchase.                                                                             損益。

      amortisation and are tested annually for impairment, or more frequently if events             並且每年進行減值測試，如果事件或情況變化
      or changes in circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other assets are            表明它們可能已減值，則更頻繁地進行測試。
      tested for impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances indicate that               當事件或情況變化表明賬面價值可能無法收回
      the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised                  時，會對其他資產進行減值測試。對於資產的
      for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable                   賬面價值超過其可收回金額的金額確認減值損
      amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs              失。可收回金額是資產的公允價值減去處置費
      of disposal and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets are            用和使用價值中的較高者。為了評估減值，將
      grouped at the lowest levels for which there are separately identifiable cash inflows         資產分類為具有可單獨識別的現金流入的最低
      which are largely independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups                 級別，這些現金流入在很大程度上獨立於來
      of assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other than goodwill that              自其他資產或資產組（現金產生單位）的現金流
      suffered an impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at the            入。除商譽外，已減值的非金融資產會在每個
      end of each reporting period.                                                                 報告期末進行減值準備的轉回。

      gains/losses – net.”                                                                          損╱收益－淨額」中的損益中確認。

      period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for each                          轄區的適用所得稅率，根據當期應納稅所得額

      where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and generate taxable income.                       表日期已頒布或實質上已頒布的稅法計算的。
      Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect                     管理層會就適用稅法受到解釋的情況定期評估
      to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation and                 納稅申報表中的頭寸，並考慮稅務機關是否有
      considers whether it is probable that a taxation authority will accept an uncertain               可能接受不確定的稅收待遇。本集團根據最可
      tax treatment. The Group measures its tax balances either based on the most likely                能的金額或預期價值計量其稅收餘額，具體取
      amount or the expected value, depending on which method provides a better                         決於哪種方法可以更好地預測不確定性的解決
      prediction of the resolution of the uncertainty.                                                  方法。

      liability in a transaction other than a business combination that at the time of the              的交易中對資產或負債的初始確認而產生的，
      transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income                則不計入遞延所得稅。該交易既不影響會計也
      tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or substantially              不影響應稅損益。遞延所得稅是使用在財務狀

2.24 Provisions                                                                                 2.24 準備

      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present legal or constructive                   當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法律或
      obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an outflow of resources will         推定義務；很可能需要有資源的流出以結算義
      be required to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.               務；及金額已被可靠估計時，當作出準備。準
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.                                      備不會就未來經營虧損確認準備。

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an outflow will            如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中有資源流
      be required in settlement is determined by considering the class of obligations as a            出的可能性，則可根據義務的類別整體考慮。
      whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to           即使在同一義務類別所包含的任何一個項目相
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be small.                            關的資源流出的可能性極低，仍須確認準備。

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s best estimate of                   撥備按管理層於報告期末償還現時義務所需開
      the expenditure required to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting           支的最佳估計的現值計量。用於確定現值的折
      period. The discount rate used to determine the present value is a pre-tax rate                 現率是稅前利率，其反映了貨幣時間價值和負
      that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks               債特定風險的當前市場評估。隨著時間的流
      specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to the passage of time is          逝，準備金的增加被確認為利息支出。
      recognised as interest expense.

                                                                                                    產的實現利得。

      carrying amount of a financial asset except for financial assets that subsequently            際利率來計算的，但隨後發生信貸減損的金融

      the loss allowance).                                                                          金後）

2.28 Leases                                                                                    2.28 租賃

      Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the           租賃在本集團可供租賃的資產使用當日確認為
      date at which the leased asset is available for use by the Group.                            使用權資產和相應的負債。

      Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group allocates               合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組成部分。本
      the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease components based on             集團根據相對獨立價格將合同中的對價分配給
      their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real estate for which the          租賃和非租賃部分。然而，對於本集團為承租
      Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-lease components             人的房地產租賃，它選擇不將租賃和非租賃組
      and instead accounts for these as a single lease component.                                  成部分分開，而是將其作為一個單獨的租賃組
                                                                                                   成部分進行會計處理。

      Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range of                租賃條款是根據個人情況協商確定的，其中包
      different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not impose any covenants             含各種不同的條款和條件。除了出租人持有的
      other than the security interests in the leased assets that are held by the lessor.          租賃資產中的擔保權益外，租賃協議不施加任
      Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                            何其他約定。租賃資產不得用作借貸擔保。

      Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a present              租賃產生的資產和負債按現值進行初始計量。
      value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of the following lease          租賃負債包括以下租賃付款額的淨現值：
      payments:

      •      fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments), less any lease                •     固定付款額（包括實質固定付款額），扣除
             incentives receivable                                                                       任何應收的租賃激勵；

      •      variable lease payment that are based on an index or a rate, initially                •     基於指數或比率確定的可變租賃付款
             measured using the index or rate as at the commencement date                                額，採用租賃期開始日的指數或比率進
                                                                                                         行初始計量；

      •      amounts expected to be payable by the Group under residual value                      •     本集團根據餘值擔保預計應付的金額；
             guarantees

      •      the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is reasonably certain to         •     本集團合理確定將行使的購買選擇權的
             exercise that option, and                                                                   行權價格；

      •      payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the lease term reflects the       •     以及在租賃期反映出本集團將行使選擇
             Group exercising that option.                                                               權的情況下終止租賃的罰款金額。

      Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension options are also                在合理的某些延期選擇權下支付的租賃付款也
      included in the measurement of the liability.                                                包括在負債的計量中。

      The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the lease. If          租賃付款使用租賃中隱含的利率折現。如果
      that rate cannot be readily determined, which is generally the case for leases in            無法輕易確定該利率（本集團中的租賃通常如
      the Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the          此），則使用承租人的增量借款利率，即單個承
      individual lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset         租人為獲得具有類似價值的資產所必需的資金
      of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment with            而必須藉入的利率在類似的經濟環境中，具有
      similar terms, security and conditions.                                                      相似的條款，安全性和條件的使用權資產。

      the related assets.

       is no material credit risk inherent in the Group’s outstanding balances of other              本集團其他應收款項的未清償餘額不存在重大
       receivables.                                                                                  的內在信用風險。

       Trade receivables and contract assets                                                          應收賬款和合同資產

       The Group applies the IFRS 9 simplified approach to measuring expected credit                  本集團採用國際財務報告準則第9號的簡化方法
       losses which uses a lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables and             計量預期信用損失，即對所有應收賬款及合同
       contract assets.                                                                               資產確認整個存續期的預期損失撥備。

       To measure the expected credit losses, trade receivables and contract assets have              為了衡量預期的信用損失，已根據共同的信用
       been grouped based on shared credit risk characteristics and the invoice days. The             風險特徵和賬齡對應收賬款和合同資產進行了
       contract assets relate to unbilled work in progress and have substantially the same            分組。合同資產與未開票的在建工程有關，並
       risk characteristics as the trade receivables within aging bucket for the same types           且具有與相同類型合同應收賬款實質上相同的
       of contracts. The Group has therefore concluded that the expected loss rates for               風險特徵。因此，小組得出結論，應收賬款的
       trade receivables are a reasonable approximation of the loss rates for the contract            預期損失率是合同資產損失率的合理近似值。
       assets.

       The expected loss rates are estimated base on the roll rate approach considering               預期損失率是基於滾動利率法，考慮過往歷史
       the invoice days of trade receivables and contract assets across historical years              年度的貿易應收款項和合同資產的發票天數以
       and the corresponding historical credit losses experienced within the period. The              及該期間的相應歷史信用損失而估計的。調整
       historical loss rates are adjusted to reflect current and forward-looking information          歷史損失率，以反映有關影響客戶清算應收款
       on macroeconomic factors affecting the ability of the customers to settle the                  能力的宏觀經濟因素的當前和前瞻性資訊。本
       receivables. The Group has identified the local GDP be the most relevant factor, and           集團已將當地GDP識別為最相關的因素，並根
       accordingly adjusts the historical loss rates based on expected changes in these               據其預期變化對歷史損失率進行調整。本集團
       factors. The Group would also monitor the counterparties individually and identify             亦會監察交易方，並識別具有不同特徵的特定
       specific counterparties with distinct characteristics for individual impairment                交易方進行个別減值評估。
       assessment.

3.3   Capital risk management                                                                3.3   資金風險管理

      The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability            本集團的資金管理政策，是保障集團能持續運
      to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns for owners and benefits           營，以為股東提供回報和為其他權利持有人提
      for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the            供利益，同時維持最佳的資本結構以降低資金
      cost of capital.                                                                             成本。

      In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the               為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調整
      amount of dividends paid to owners, return capital to shareholders, issue new                支付予股東的股利數額、向股東退還資本、發
      shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                                        行新股或出售資產以減低債務。

      Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital on the basis              與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用資本負債比
      of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital.         率監察其資本。此比率按照債務淨額除以總資
      Net debt is calculated as total borrowings (including “current and non–current               本計算。債務淨額為總借款（包括合併財務狀況
      borrowings” as shown in the consolidated statement of financial position) less               表所列的「流動及非流動借款」
                                                                                                                ）減去現金和現金
      cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is calculated as “equity” as shown in               等價物。總資本為「權益」
                                                                                                              （如合併財務狀況表所
      the consolidated statement of financial position plus net debt.                              列）
                                                                                                    加債務淨額。

      The gearing ratios at 31 December 2021 and 2020 were as follows:                             在二零二一年及二零二零年十二月三十一日，

      of the properties.                                                                            餘價值的預期反映在房地產的公允價值中。

      Valuation processes of the Group                                                              本集團的估值流程

      The Group’s finance department includes a team that reviews the valuations                    本集團財務部設有一個小組，專責就財務報告
      performed by the independent valuers for financial reporting purposes. This                   目的對獨立估值師的估值進行審查。此小組直
      team reports directly to the chief financial officer (CFO). Discussions of valuation          接向首席財務官(CFO)匯報。為配合本集團的中
      processes and results are held among the CFO, the valuation team and the                      期和年度報告日期，CFO、估值小組與估值師
      valuers at least once every six months, in line with the Group’s interim and annual           最少每六個月開會一次，討論估值流程和相關
      reporting dates.                                                                              結果。

      At the end of each reporting period the finance department:                                   在每個財政年度末，財務部將會：

      –      Verifies all major inputs to the independent valuation report;                         －   核實獨立估值報告的所有重要資料來
                                                                                                        源；

             valuation report;                                                                          的變動；

      Valuation techniques                                                                                            估值技術

      For completed office buildings, the valuation was determined using income                                       已落成辦公大樓的估值利用收益法，按重大不
      approach based on significant unobservable inputs. These inputs include:                                        可觀察輸入釐定。這些輸入包括：


                                                                                                              Range of                        Relationship of unobservable inputs to
      Unobservable inputs    Description                                                                      unobservable inputs             fair value
      不可觀察輸入                 描述                                                                               不可觀察輸入的範圍                       不可觀察輸入對公允價值的關係


      Term yields            Based on yields extracting from terms of current leases                          5% (2020: 5%)                   The higher the term yields rate, the lower the
                                                                                                                                                 fair value
      租期內收益率                 根據當前已簽訂的租賃合約條款，所能獲取的收益率                                                          5%（二零二零年：5%）                    租期內收益率越高，公允價值越低

      The interest accrued was due within 12 months and presented in interest                       近。本年計提的應收利息在12個月內到期，並
      receivables.                                                                                  列賬於應收利息中。

      The Group considers the probability of default upon initial recognition of asset and          本集團考慮在資產最初確認時發生違約的可能
      whether there has been a significant increase in credit risk on an ongoing basis              性，以及在整個報告期間信貸風險是否持續顯
      throughout each reporting period. To assess whether there is a significant increase           著增加。為了評估信用風險是否顯著增加，本
      in credit risk, the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the asset as at         集團將資產在報告日期發生違約的風險與初始
      the reporting date with the risk of default as at the date of initial recognition.            確認日期發生違約的風險進行比較。

      The Group performed expected credit loss assessment of loans to third parties                 本集團通過對具有類似信用風險特徵的貸款進
      collectively by grouping loans with similar credit risk characteristics. During the           行分組，共同對第三方貸款進行了預期的信用
      years ended 31 December 2021 and 2020, the majority of the loans were in stage 1              損失評估。在截至二零二一年十二月三十一日
      and there was no significant change in credit quality for loans for both years.               和二零二零年十二月三十一日的年度中，大多
                                                                                                    數貸款處於第一階段，並且這兩年的貸款信用
                                                                                                    質量均未發生重大變化。

31    Income tax (credit)/expense (Continued)                                               31   所得稅沖回（續）

      (a)   No provision for profits tax in the Cayman Islands and Hong Kong has                 (a)   由於本集團於該等年度內在開曼群島或
            been made as the Group has no assessable profits for the years in those                    香港並無應課稅收入，故於該等地區並
            jurisdictions.                                                                             無作出該等司法權區的所得稅準備。

      (b)   According to regulation (Gao Ji [2022] No. 390) issued by the National               (b)   根據國家發展和改革委員會等部门頒發
            Development and Reform Commission, the entity can register for the                         的高技[2022]390號文，企業如果滿足相關
            National Important Software Enterprise (“NISE”) in tax bureau if the entity                條件可以在稅務局備案成為重點軟件企

35   Transactions with non-controlling interests                                             35   與非控制性權益的交易

     Disposal of interest in subsidiaries without loss of control                                 不喪失控制權地處置子公司權益

     In November 2021, Shenzhen Wojiacloud Network Technology Co., Ltd.                           於二零二一年十一月，本集團的附屬公司深圳
     (“Wojiacloud”), a subsidiary of the Group, received a capital injection of                   我家雲網絡科技有限公司(「我家雲」)從第三方公
     RMB30,000,000 from a third party. The Group’s equity interest in Wojiacloud was              司獲得注資人民幣30,000,000元。 上述注資後，
     diluted from 80% to 68% after the above capital injection and the Group continues            本集團於我家雲的股權由80％稀釋至68％，由

(i)    Accounting policy choice for non-controlling interests                                     (i)    少數股東的會計政策選擇

       The Group recognises non-controlling interests in an acquired entity either at fair               本集團以公允價值或非控股權益在被收購實體
       value or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s           的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比例確認被收購實體
       net identifiable assets. This decision is made on an acquisition-by-acquisition                   的非控制性權益。 該決定是在逐個收購的基礎
       basis. For the non-controlling interests in Yunji, the Group elected to recognise                 上做出的。 對於雲集的非控股權益，本集團選
       the non-controlling interests at its proportionate share of the acquired net                      擇按照其在取得的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比例
       identifiable assets. See Note 2.3 for the Group’s accounting policies for business                確認非控股權益。本集團企業合併的會計政策
       combinations.                                                                                     見附註2.3。
